# Claim ledger: limits L4 and L5 (round 1 blind audit, 26 Nov 2026)

Scope: `app/content/courses/limits/` l13 to l20, review-4, review-5, homework-4, homework-5, problems-4, problems-5 and fluency-l13 (Stage 1, full depth), at commit `6590e79e`. Every row was read and judged by hand. The basis column gives the derivation the row was recomputed from, then the source. "script" means a named check in `docs/reports/limits-L4-5-concepts.py` (sympy 1.14, NumPy 2.5, SciPy 1.18: newer than CI's pins; no figure depends on the version).

Paths are shortened: `s3` is `screens[3]`, `q1` is `quiz[1]`, `i4` is `items[4]`; `w` is `widget`, `fb` is `feedback.byKey`. A slip row covers the `wrong[]` value and its feedback line together; a hints row covers every rung of the screen. Rows: 1604 (1584 correct, 16 imprecise, 4 wrong).

Sources named: Spivak, *Calculus* (4th ed.), ch. 11, 12 and 20; Stewart, *Calculus* (8th ed.); Hull, *Options, Futures and Other Derivatives*, ch. 4; Kelly (1956).

| l20 | summary.ideas[0] | $f'' > 0$ means the slope is rising and the graph bends up. $f'' < 0$ means it bends down. | correct | Spivak ch. 11 appendix (convexity and concavity); Stewart 4.3, 4.5 |
